The pricing-enforcement routine now makes that exclusion durable on every margin save: priced source rows refresh from their verified cost, while any row lacking a positive cost/list basis is automatically removed from availability and publication, retaining its source record for later audit/pricing. The Design Tool's former Madison source-list assertion was corrected to require verified landed cost plus the saved homeowner margin. Post-remediation audit: **17,448** public SKU rows; `0` without a positive homeowner, contractor, or wholesale price; `0` public unpriced rows. Direct public B15 pricing returns Madison homeowner price **$269.85** from its landed cost, not its `$694.00` list price. Validation: 21 focused pricing/margin/admin/Designer regressions, TypeScript, production build, SQL reconciliation, and clean diff integrity passed.
- [x] Close the **Designer published-price gate**. `design.priceForCabinetsByStyles` now requires every returned supplier line to be both available **and published**, matching the public storefront/catalog truth gate. An unavailable or unpublished line cannot re-enter a plan price/takeoff merely because it still has an old source cost in the database. Regression temporarily unpublished the live Madison 3DB24 source row, confirmed the Designer returns no price, and restored the record; normal published B15/Madison margin pricing remains covered. Validation: 13 focused Designer pricing/surface/sell-price regressions, TypeScript, production build, local Designer/health HTTP 200, and clean diff integrity passed.
- [x] Lock **Pricing & Margins** to the actual owner-defined formula rather than showing a misleading per-supply-line markup field. The inactive distributor-premium editor and its mutation were removed because the shared sell-price resolver correctly applies only the saved homeowner/contractor/wholesale tier markups to verified landed cost. There are currently no nonzero distributor-premium records. The admin query now returns only the tier settings it truly controls, and regressions prove the obsolete UI/API are absent while tier settings remain admin-only. Validation: 15 focused pricing/admin regressions, TypeScript, production build, local admin/Designer/health HTTP 200, and clean diff integrity passed.

Validation: compact workspace, mobile attributes, toolbar layout, and mobile toolbar regressions; TypeScript; production build; 1280px tablet Designer preview; and diff integrity passed.

It now resolves every public style label as `aliasName` first with `ourName` only as a fallback, so the product page, Designer, cart, and materials-list surface the same verified public style label. The B24 / style `390020` regression confirms `Air Dawn (Preston)` is returned and never `Slim White Oak`; source identity, SKU linkage, availability, cost, and tier pricing were not changed. Validation: 4 focused public-style/affiliate-style regressions, TypeScript, production build, and clean diff integrity passed.
- [x] Repair the **Preston Air Dawn toe-kick price handoff**. The selected `TK-8(TKC)` record is active, available, published, and has verified source list `$46.00`, landed cost `$16.10`, homeowner price `$25.44`, contractor price `$20.77`, and wholesale price `$20.13`. Designer had asked only for shorthand `TK-8`, so its Materials & Parts list showed the line as `Price pending` even though the real source row existed. The trim resolver now accepts the verified `TK-8(TKC)` source-code variant while retaining the shared shorthand for supplier lines that truly use it; cart, priced subtotal, and Materials & Parts all carry the returned source code and tier price rather than a made-up substitute. - [x] Add a conservative **UPS/FedEx/USPS parcel eligibility and live-rate path**. Only a carton with supplier-authoritative package length, width, height, and packaged weight can be considered; UPS/FedEx enforce 150 lb / 108 in length / 165 in length-plus-girth, USPS enforces 70 lb / 130 in length-plus-girth and marks 108–130 in as oversized. Eligible all-carton groups request live EasyPost parcel rates; carton-by-carton actual services and prices are displayed in the cart/checkout. Missing, oversized, non-EasyPost, or distributor-fixed-rule groups remain on the existing live LTL route—never an invented parcel quote. Official source references live in `docs/PARCEL_CARRIER_LIMITS_SOURCES.md`. Validation: 38 focused catalog, parcel-rule, EasyPost, freight, cart, and checkout regressions; TypeScript; production build; local health/cart/admin HTTP 200; diff integrity. Current Operations configuration is WWEX live mode without stored credentials, so parcel shopping activates only after an authorized EasyPost key and carrier accounts are configured; no key or carrier setup was guessed.

Validation: focused catalog/carton/parcel/admin regressions, TypeScript, production build, local health/admin HTTP 200, and clean diff integrity.
